# Derived coverings

Built from published coverings by the constructions described in [derived/](../derived/README.md): products over a split of the points, complement duality and one-point extensions. Each entry names every component covering used; every one is on the Covering Repository (at this size or better). The component files are not included here: download them from the Repository, check them against the sha1 hashes in [data/components.tsv](../data/components.tsv), then rebuild with `python derived/build.py v,k,t,m`. Plain coverings are the case m = t. "Status" shows the table on 2026-10-25.
